Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The spectrum variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 122 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 942 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_122(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 122. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^942(S, F) is trivial. QED.
